# Coalroot
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Mutations, Machine Fuels
[Coalroot]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: COALROOT
Tooltip: Machine Fuel; Adds +5,000 Fuel to a refuelable machine.; A smoldering root that oddly can also be used as fuel.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; UNCOMMON MUTATION
A Coalroot is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Coalroot can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 5 [[Ashwreath]] and 3 [[Scourroot]] within 1 block of farmland.
While growing, a Coalroot needs water and has 8 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it increases [[Farming]] experience by 20%.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Coalroots also drop 510 (7 stacks plus 62) [[Potato]], 1,190 (18 stacks plus 38) [[Carrot]], 510 [[Nether Wart]] and +2,720 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Coalroot can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 80,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 40 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Coalroot grants an additional 500 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Coalroots are used to create a [[Chloronite]].
### Fuel
Coalroot adds 5,000 Fuel to [[Drills]] when combined in a [[Drill Anvil]] or to the [[Composter]] when inserted.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])|DNA Analysis Milestone III (3) ([[Crop Analyzer]])|1x Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]) [[Coalroot]] 4x Coalroot 4x [[Creambloom]] 8x [[Chocoberry]]|Recipe: [[Chocoberry]] x2 ×4 + [[Creambloom]] x2 ×2 + Coalroot x2 ×2 + [[Mutations Sack]] → [[Mutations Sack]]|
## Bugs
- Coalroot is difficult to harvest using a [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]] due to the [[Dead Bush]] base. It can be successfully harvested by quickly breaking the head part of the plant after breaking the dead bush.
- Attempting to harvest Coalroot with [[Delicate]] applied causes the [[Dead Bush]] part of its model to be replanted.
## Gallery
- Coalroot in the Greenhouse Coalroot in the [[Greenhouse]]
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Coalroot.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Coalroot.|

# Compactor
Categories: Uncommon items, Minion upgrades, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Cobblestone Collection, Minion Modifiers
[Compactor]
Type: Minion Upgrade ([[Minion Upgrades]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Cobblestone V (5) ([[Cobblestone]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 1,120x (17 stacks plus 32 (1,120 in total)) [[Cobblestone]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Redstone ([[Redstone Dust]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: COMPACTOR
Tooltip: Minion Upgrade; This item can be used as a minion upgrade. This will automatically turn materials that a minion produces into their block form.; Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
Compactor is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Minion Upgrades|Minion Upgrade]] item unlocked at [[Cobblestone|Cobblestone V (5)]]. It turns the resources a minion produces into their block form. Some minions are not able to have this upgrade equipped, such as [[Potato Minion|Potato Minions]], [[Carrot Minion|Carrot Minions]], etc.
The compactor compresses items only into an existing vanilla Minecraft block form (if one exists); turning them to their enchanted forms requires a [[Super Compactor 3000]].
## Obtaining
### Quest
This item can be obtained by completing the quest that requires giving a [[Lapis Pickaxe]] to the [[Lapis Miner]].
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Cobblestone V (5) ([[Cobblestone]])|1x [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 7x [[Enchanted Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] ×7 + [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] → Compactor|
## Usage
### Minion Upgrade
The Compactor turns the resources a [[Minions|Minion]] produces into their block form. Compactor can be placed in the minions listed below.
|Minion|Item|Block|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
[Farming]
|[[Melon Minion]]|[[Melon Slice]]|[[Melon]]|Warning: Melons cannot be uncombined back to Melon Slices (without breaking the block, which takes time and loses some slices).|
|[[Mushroom Minion]]|Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|[[Red Mushroom Block]]|Warning: Red Mushroom Block cannot be uncombined back to Red Mushrooms (without breaking the block, which takes time and loses some mushrooms).|
|[[Mushroom Minion]]|Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|[[Brown Mushroom Block]]|Warning: Brown Mushroom Block cannot be uncombined back to Brown Mushrooms (without breaking the block, which takes time and loses some mushrooms).|
[Combat]
|[[Slime Minion]]|[[Slimeball]]|[[Slime Block]]||
[Mining]
|[[Coal Minion]]|[[Coal]]|[[Block of Coal]]||
|[[Iron Minion]]|[[Iron Ingot]]|[[Block of Iron]]|Requires the minion to also use an [[Auto Smelter]]|
|[[Gold Minion]]|[[Gold Ingot]]|[[Block of Gold]]|Requires the minion to also use an [[Auto Smelter]]|
|[[Diamond Minion]]|[[Diamond]]|[[Block of Diamond]]||
|[[Lapis Minion]]|[[Lapis Lazuli]]|[[Block of Lapis Lazuli]]||
|[[Redstone Minion]]|[[Redstone Dust]]|[[Block of Redstone]]||
|[[Emerald Minion]]|[[Emerald]]|[[Block of Emerald]]||
|[[Quartz Minion]]|[[Nether Quartz]]|[[Block of Quartz]]|Warning: Blocks of Quartz cannot be uncombined back to quartz. However, the blocks can still be used to craft Enchanted Nether Quartz.|
|[[Glowstone Minion]]|[[Glowstone Dust]]|[[Glowstone]]|Warning: Glowstone cannot be uncombined back to Glowstone Dust (without breaking the block, which takes time and loses some dust). However, the blocks can still be used to craft Enchanted Glowstone Dust.|
|[[Ice Minion]]|[[Ice]]|[[Packed Ice]]|Warning: Packed Ice cannot be uncombined back to ice. However, Packed Ice can still be used to craft Enchanted Ice.|
|[[Snow Minion]]|[[Snowball]]|[[Snow Block]]||
[Fishing]
|[[Clay Minion]]|[[Clay Ball]]|[[Clay]]|[[Auto Smelter]] takes priority over Compactor for newly harvested clay.; Warning: Clay cannot be uncombined back to Clay Balls, but Clay can be broken to obtain the Clay Balls. Clay can be used to create Enchanted Clay Balls|
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Super Compactor 3000]]|Cobblestone X (10) ([[Cobblestone]])|[[Compactor]] 1x Compactor 32x [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 224x (3 stacks plus 32 (224 in total)) [[Enchanted Cobblestone]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Cobblestone]] x32 ×7 + Compactor + [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] x32 → [[Super Compactor 3000]]|
## Trivia
- Because Compactors use the same default skin as [[Super Compactor 3000|Super Compactor 3000s]], they are commonly used to scam players.
- The Compactor shares a recipe with the [[Dropper]] (from Vanilla Minecraft) but uses enchanted items (cobblestone, redstone).
- Despite being able to make drops that can be compacted with a Compactor in other minions, a Compactor cannot be placed in [[Revenant Minion|Revenant Minions]], [[Voidling Minion|Voidling Minions]], [[Inferno Minion|Inferno Minions]] and [[Tarantula Minion|Tarantula Minions]].
- Putting a [[Diamond Spreading]] in a minion does not make Compactors usable in the minion if it couldn't be used before.

|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Added "Right-click to view recipes!" to the lore.|

Item ID: COMPOST
Tooltip: Can be spread across a Garden plot with a Sprayonator or used in Pest Traps.; Attracts: Earthworm, Mosquito; Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
Compost is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item. Both it and its compact form, the [[Compost Bundle]], are used to unlock plots in [[The Garden]]. Some [[The Garden|Garden Visitors]] also request Compost. Compost is also used with a [[Sprayonator]] to spray plots and attract [[Mosquito|Mosquitos]] and [[Earthworm|Earthworms]].
## Obtaining
### Composter
Compost is obtained by using the [[Composter]], which needs 4000 [[Composter|Organic Matter]] to make each Compost, excluding [[Composter|Composter Upgrades]]. The following table gives the Organic Matter for all known compostable items. All Organic Matter values below are exact. For example, Potatoes are worth exactly 0.33 organic matter, not 1/3. Note that [[Condensed Fermento]] and [[Condensed Helianthus]] can only be used with sufficient [[Composter|Composter Upgrades]] because they are worth more than the default limit of 40,000 Organic Matter.
Compost also has a 10% chance to drop from killing any [[Pests|Pest]]. However, due to it sharing a drop pool with other items, its effective drop chance is 1.67% (1 in 60 kills) (not affected by [[Farming Fortune]]). In case of the [[Field Mouse]], the chance is 100%.
The best item to buy from the [[Bazaar]] for making Compost is currently [[Enchanted Seeds]] at 11,838 [[Coins]] per 4000 Organic Matter.

### Attributes
[[Attributes|Visitor Compost]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants up to 2.5% chance for [[Garden Visitors|Visitors]] to bring Compost, which can be further increased with [[Attributes|Queenly Echo]] attribute.
[[Attributes|Sprayonator Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants 10% chance for [[Pests]] to drop an extra [[Sprayonator|Sprayonator Material]].
## Usage
### Garden Plots
Compost is used to purchase [[The Garden|Garden]] plots.
### Garden Visitors
[[Geonathan Greatforge]] and [[Jotraeline Greatforge]] request Compost when visiting in [[The Garden]].
### Pests
Compost can be used with a [[Sprayonator]] to attract [[Earthworm|Earthworms]] and [[Mosquito|Mosquitos]] to a sprayed plot.
Compost can also be used as bait in a [[Pest Trap]], [[Mouse Trap]], or [[Vermin Trap]], allowing them to catch [[Pests]] and increasing the chance to catch a [[Earthworm]] or a [[Mosquito]] by 12x.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Compost Bundle]]|None|[[Compost]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Compost|Recipe: Compost x32 ×7 → [[Compost Bundle]]|

# Cornucopia
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Cornucopia]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Garden Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CORNUCOPIA
Tooltip: Dropped rarely from Wheat during the Harvest Feast event.; Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
Cornucopia is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item dropped during the [[Harvest Feast]] used to craft [[Feast Burger with a Side of Deepfries]], [[Not Very Moldy Bread]], and the [[Cornucopia Crystal]].
## Obtaining
### Farming
Cornucopia has a 0.006% (1 in 18,000 crops) chance to drop when breaking mature [[Wheat]], a 15% chance to drop from a [[Fly]] and a 30% chance to drop from a [[Field Mouse]] while the [[Harvest Feast]] is ongoing and the crop is in-season.
[[Overbloom]] is used to increase the drop chance.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Not Very Moldy Bread]]|None|16x [[Fine Flour]] [[Cornucopia]] 32x Cornucopia|Recipe: Cornucopia x4 ×8 + [[Fine Flour]] x16 → [[Not Very Moldy Bread]]|
|[[Cornucopia Crystal]]|None|1x [[Farm Crystal]] [[Cornucopia]] 64x Cornucopia|Recipe: Cornucopia x8 ×8 + [[Farm Crystal]] → [[Cornucopia Crystal]]|

# Creambloom
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Mutations
[Creambloom]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CREAMBLOOM
Tooltip: A flower so soft and creamy, it could almost pass for milk. Just don't be tempted to try drinking it.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; UNCOMMON MUTATION
A Creambloom is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Creambloom can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 8 [[Choconut]] within 1 block of farmland.
While growing, a Creambloom needs water and has 6 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it grants immunity to negative effects.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Creamblooms also drop 2,240 (35 stacks) [[Cocoa Beans]] and +4,480 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Creambloom can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 60,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 30 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Creambloom grants an additional 500 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Creamblooms are used to create a [[Cheesebite]] and [[Snoozling]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])|DNA Analysis Milestone III (3) ([[Crop Analyzer]])|1x Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]) 4x [[Coalroot]] [[Creambloom]] 4x Creambloom 8x [[Chocoberry]]|Recipe: [[Chocoberry]] x2 ×4 + Creambloom x2 ×2 + [[Coalroot]] x2 ×2 + [[Mutations Sack]] → [[Mutations Sack]]|
|[[Trunk Polish]]|None|1x [[Glass Bottle]] [[Creambloom]] 4x Creambloom 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Melon Juice]]|Recipe: [[Melon Juice]] x32 ×4 + Creambloom ×4 + [[Glass Bottle]] → [[Trunk Polish]]|
## Gallery
- Creambloom in the Greenhouse Creambloom in the [[Greenhouse]]

The Cricket Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Pest Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Cricket Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Cricket]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Cricket Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Cricket Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Pest Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Pest Fortune ([[Attributes]]); Grants +5–50 [[Farming Fortune]] on Pest ([[Pest (Mob Type)]]).
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

A Cricket Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

# Cropie
Aliases: Croppie
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Cropie]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Garden Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 25,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CROPIE
Tooltip: Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
A Cropie is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item used to craft [[Cropie Armor]] and [[Cropie Talisman]].
## Obtaining
### Farming
A Cropie has a chance to drop when breaking a mature [[Wheat]], [[Carrot]], or [[Potato]] crop while wearing at least 2 pieces of [[Tater Armor]], [[Fermento Armor]], or [[Helianthus Armor]].
- Different armor pieces do not count towards each other's Tiered Bonuses.
- The chance can be increased by [[Overbloom]].
Pieces: Chance
2/4: 0.03% (1 in 3,333.3 crops broken)
3/4: 0.04% (1 in 2,500.0 crops broken)
4/4: 0.05% (1 in 2,000.0 crops broken)
### Greenhouse
A Cropie has a chance to drop when breaking any crop or mutation with a "Harvestable" status inside the [[Greenhouse]] while wearing at least 2 pieces of [[Tater Armor]]..
- The chance can be increased by [[Overbloom]].
Pieces: Chance
2/4: 12% (1 in 8.33 crops broken)
3/4: 16% (1 in 6.25 crops broken)
4/4: 20% (1 in 5 crops broken)
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Cropie Helmet ([[Cropie Armor]])|None|1x Tater Helmet ([[Tater Armor]]) 6x [[Box of Seeds]] 8x [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] [[Cropie]] 20x Cropie|Recipe: Cropie x10 ×2 + [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] x8 + [[Box of Seeds]] x3 ×2 + [[Tater Armor]] → [[Cropie Armor]]|
|Cropie Chestplate ([[Cropie Armor]])|None|1x Tater Chestplate ([[Tater Armor]]) 6x [[Box of Seeds]] 8x [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] [[Cropie]] 20x Cropie 45x [[Enchanted Baked Potato]]|Recipe: Cropie x10 ×2 + [[Tater Armor]] + [[Box of Seeds]] x3 ×2 + [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] x8 + [[Enchanted Baked Potato]] x15 ×3 → [[Cropie Armor]]|
|Cropie Leggings ([[Cropie Armor]])|None|1x Tater Leggings ([[Tater Armor]]) 6x [[Box of Seeds]] 8x [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] [[Cropie]] 20x Cropie 30x [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]]|Recipe: Cropie x10 ×2 + [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] x8 + [[Box of Seeds]] x3 ×2 + [[Tater Armor]] + [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]] x15 ×2 → [[Cropie Armor]]|
|Cropie Boots ([[Cropie Armor]])|None|1x Tater Boots ([[Tater Armor]]) 16x [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] [[Cropie]] 20x Cropie|Recipe: Cropie x10 ×2 + [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] x8 ×2 + [[Tater Armor]] → [[Cropie Armor]]|
### Composter
Cropie can be used to fill up the [[Composter]]'s Crop Meter by 2.5k Organic Matter.
It can also be used to purchase [[Composter|Composter Upgrades]] tiers 8 to 13
|Tier|Amount required|
|-|-|
|8|[[Cropie]] 3 Cropie|
|9|[[Cropie]] 6 Cropie|
|10|[[Cropie]] 12 Cropie|
|11|[[Cropie]] 24 Cropie|
|12|[[Cropie]] 48 Cropie|
|13|[[Cropie]] 96 (1 stack plus 32) Cropie|
## Bugs
- Cropie does not drop from harvesting [[Greenhouse]] crops if wearing [[Fermento Armor]] or [[Helianthus Armor]].

# Crystalized Moonlight
Aliases: Crystallized Moonlight
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Crystalized Moonlight]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No

[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CRYSTALIZED_MOONLIGHT
Tooltip: Dropped rarely from Moonflower during the Harvest Feast event.; Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
Crystalized Moonlight is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item dropped during the [[Harvest Feast]] used to craft [[Moondew]].
## Obtaining
### Farming
Crystalized Moonlight has a 0.006% (1 in 18,000 crops) chance to drop when breaking mature [[Moonflower]], a 15% chance to drop from a [[Firefly]] and a 30% chance to drop from a [[Field Mouse]] while the [[Harvest Feast]] is ongoing and the crop is in season.
[[Overbloom]] is used to increase the drop chance.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Moondew]]|None|[[Crystalized Moonlight]] 2x Crystalized Moonlight 16x [[Enchanted End Stone]]|Recipe: Crystalized Moonlight ×2 + [[Enchanted End Stone]] x16 → [[Moondew]]|
## Tips
- Players going for Crystalized Moonlight can use the /islandtime command to quickly switch their garden to daytime before killing [[Firefly|Fireflies]] to gain additional [[Overbloom]] from the [[Sunset]] enchantment.

Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Cuboa Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Echo of Boxes]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:

As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Day Saver
Categories: Uncommon items, Blocks, Craftable items, Confirmations needed, Pages with UIs, More info needed, Nether Quartz Collection, Time Saver
[Day Saver]
Type: Block
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Nether Quartz III (3) ([[Nether Quartz]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 35 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 3x [[Sand]] 15x [[Nether Quartz]] 3x [[Sunflower]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DAY_SAVER
Tooltip: Control the time on your private island when paired with a Night Saver.; UNCOMMON
The Day Saver is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] block that is unlocked at [[Nether Quartz|Nether Quartz III (3)]]. It can be used to unlock the Island Time menu on a [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]] to make it always day time on the island.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Nether Quartz III (3) ([[Nether Quartz]])|3x [[Sunflower]] 3x [[Glass]] 15x [[Nether Quartz]]|Recipe: [[Glass]] ×3 + [[Nether Quartz]] x5 ×3 + [[Sunflower]] ×3 → Day Saver|
## Usage
A Day Saver followed by a [[Night Saver]] can be used to unlock the Island Time menu on a [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]], after which there will be an option to set the time in one hour increments, as well as whether to freeze the time.
It prevents mobs that generate naturally at night from spawning. However, if it's activated during the night, mobs will briefly keep spawning; exiting and re-entering the island solves this.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
It can be used with a [[Solar Panel]] to constantly grant [[Minions]] +25% speed, similarly to an [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]]. However, the Day Saver doesn't work when the player is offline, so they won't receive the benefits of the Solar Panel then.
## Interface
The Day Saver originally featured its own interface when placed down. However, after the 0.19.9 update, this was merged with the [[Night Saver]] and replaced by the Island Time menu found under the Island Properties menu on the [[Private Island]] or within the [[The Garden|Desk]] inside [[The Garden]]
## Chat Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Placed on [[Private Island]]; After 0.19.9 Update|You can't place this! Unlock this feature in the Island Properties menu. CLICK HERE|
|Placed on [[The Garden]]; After 0.19.9 Update|You can't place this! Unlock this feature in the Desk menu. CLICK HERE|
|Placed on [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]]; Before 0.19.9 Update|You placed a Day Saver! It will now always be day on your private island! RIGHT CLICK the Day Saver to change its settings!|
|Setting time; Before 0.19.9 Update|Your Day Saver's time has been set to time!|
|Attempting to place when a [[Night Saver]] is already present; Before 0.19.9 Update|You can only place 1 of these on your island at a time!|
## Trivia
- [[Glitched Items|Nulls]] of certain blocks can be created by using a Day Saver or Night Saver.[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 26, 2019|0.4|Added Day Saver.|
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|The Day Saver can select the exact time to set when right-clicked.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Limited the Day Savers to only 1 on private islands.|
|Dec 12, 2023|0.19.9|Changed Day Saver from a placed block to a [[Settings]] feature.|

# Dead Plant
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items
[Dead Plant]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes

Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 500 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DEAD_PLANT
Tooltip: Once full of life, now a reminder of your terrible gardening skills.; UNCOMMON
A Dead Plant is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
A Dead Plant replaces plants in the [[Greenhouse]] if they reach -100 water. Alternatively, the decay mechanic will turn most fully grown mutation into Dead Plants after a few days, even if fully hydrated or placed by the player. Different mutations can have different decay timers. The lowest is 3 days, and certain mutations do not have any decay at all. This can be observed using the [[Plant Diagnostics Tool]].
Dead Plants can decay as well: they have a decay timer of 72 hours and a minimum mutation value of 10, and leave nothing in their place when they decay.
## Usage
### Mutations
Dead Plants are used to create a [[Witherbloom]] and [[Zombud]].
## Trivia
- Despite not being a [[Mutations|Mutation]], Dead Plant can be stored in the [[Mutations Sack|Mutations Sacks]].
## History

Deepfries are an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item dropped during the [[Harvest Feast]] used to craft the [[Hashbrown]].
## Obtaining
### Farming
Deepfries have a 0.006% (1 in 18,000 crops) chance to drop when breaking mature [[Potato|Potatoes]], a 15% chance to drop from a [[Locust]] and a 30% chance to drop from a [[Field Mouse]] while the [[Harvest Feast]] is ongoing and the crop is in season.
[[Overbloom]] is used to increase the drop chance.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Hashbrown]]|None|8x [[Sunflower Oil]] [[Deepfries]] 12x Deepfries 16x [[Enchanted Baked Potato]]|Recipe: Deepfries x2 ×6 + [[Sunflower Oil]] x4 ×2 + [[Enchanted Baked Potato]] x16 → [[Hashbrown]]|
|[[Feast Burger with a Side of Deepfries]]|None|12x [[Feastfungus]] [[Deepfries]] 12x Deepfries 12x [[Cornucopia]] 24x [[Enchanted Hay Bale]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Hay Bale]] x6 ×4 + Deepfries x6 ×2 + [[Feastfungus]] x6 ×2 + [[Cornucopia]] x12 → [[Feast Burger with a Side of Deepfries]]|
### Garden Visitor
When visiting [[The Garden]], [[Taylor]] can request 256 Deepfries in exchange for the [[Barn Skins|Fast Food Barn Skin]].

A Defuse Kit is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item obtained from [[Dungeon Secrets]].
## Obtaining
Defuse Kits can be obtained from [[Dungeon Secrets]].
## Usage
A Defuse Kit is used to defuse Traps by breaking the string placed between tripwire traps. Players receive increased [[Intelligence]] from how many traps they have defused, capping at +10: this boost affects your profile, and thus will not change when switching Defuse Kits.

## Obtaining
Desert Crystal spawns on [[Desert Island]] placement by the player on their [[Private Island]].

When placed on the [[Private Island]], a Desert Crystal grows one of [[Sugar Cane]], [[Cactus]], or [[Dead Bush]] on any [[Sand]] within 16 blocks of the Crystal. It performs every 30 seconds with a maximum of 7 plants grown.

# Dirt Bottle
Aliases: Dirty

[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes

[Shop]
Merchants: [[Smithmonger]]
Buy: 100,000 [[Coins]]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DIRT_BOTTLE
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Dirty reforge when combined with a Melee Weapon or Fishing Rod.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Strength  Attack Speed  Ferocity; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 10.; UNCOMMON REFORGE STONE
The Dirt Bottle is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Dirty]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Weapons|Melee Weapon]] or a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], granting [[Strength]], [[Attack Speed]], and [[Ferocity]].
## Obtaining
The Dirt Bottle can be purchased from the [[Smithmonger]] for 100,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Dirt Bottle can be applied to [[Swords]], [[Longswords]], [[Gemstone Gauntlet]], and [[Fishing Rods]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Dirty]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]], [[Attack Speed]] and [[Ferocity]], which scale with the weapon's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining X (10)]]. A weapon that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Dirty]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+2 Str ([[Strength]]) +2 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +2 Fer ([[Ferocity]])|+4 Str ([[Strength]]) +3 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +3 Fer ([[Ferocity]])|+6 Str ([[Strength]]) +5 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +6 Fer ([[Ferocity]])|+10 Str ([[Strength]]) +10 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +9 Fer ([[Ferocity]])|+12 Str ([[Strength]]) +15 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +12 Fer ([[Ferocity]])|+15 Str ([[Strength]]) +20 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +15 Fer ([[Ferocity]])|+20 Str ([[Strength]]) +25 AS ([[Attack Speed]]) +18 Fer ([[Ferocity]])|
|Cost to apply|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- [[Reforging|Dirty]] is the only [[Reforging|Reforge]] that grants [[Ferocity]].
- The Dirt Bottle can be applied on a [[Unripe Jerryseed]] and given to [[Dirt Guy]] to obtain a [[Fertilized Jerryseed]].

# Dirt Rod
Categories: Uncommon items, Fishing Rods, Fishing, Tools
[Dirt Rod]
Type: Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Stats: [[Fishing Speed]]: 20
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DIRT_ROD
Tooltip: Fishing Speed: +20; This rod allows you to fish on dirt.; At least you won't get wet!; This item has no purchasing data yet!; ථ Hook NONE ꨃ Line NONE ࿉ Sinker NONE; Talk to Roddy in the Backwater Bayou to apply parts to this rod.; This item can be reforged!; UNCOMMON FISHING ROD
The Dirt Rod is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]] that allows the player to fish in [[Dirt]]. It gives +20 [[Fishing Speed]]. It displays the buyer's name, cost paid, and the details about in which auction it was sold and the ranking of the buyer within that auction. This data disappears if the item is traded in any way.
## Obtaining
The Dirt Rod can sometimes appear on [[Shen's Auction|Shen's Special]], and players can bid on it. The top bidders win the item.
## Usage
### Fishing
Fishing Rod:
Dirt Rod [[Fishing Speed]]: +20
[Effects]
[This rod allows you to fish on dirt.]
The Dirt Rod can be used to fish on Dirt blocks. The player can catch [[Dirt]] or [[Worm the Fish]], which has a 0.5% chance to be caught. If used in water, it will display "This rod wasn't made for this!" in the chat, but it will work. Like any water rod, it will not work in lava.
## Trivia
- It is impossible to fish in [[Coarse Dirt]] or [[Grass]]. But it is possible to fish on [[Farmland]].
- Caught [[Dirt]] isn't actually given to the player.
- It is possible to dirt-fish in [[Dungeons]], despite [[Fishing]] in Water or Lava being disabled in Dungeons.

Displaced Leech is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Power Stones|Power Stone]], which unlocks the [[Power Stones|Sanguisuge]] power for [[Accessories]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|

## Requirements
The Displaced Leech requires [[Combat|Combat X (10)]] to be used and can only be used as a Power Stone for [[Accessories]]. Combining 9 Displaced Leeches at [[Maxwell]] will permanently unlock the Sanguisuge [[Power Stones|Power]] for [[Accessories]].

Dragon Sack is a [[Sacks|Sack]] unlocked in the [[Ender Pearl]] collection at [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearl IV (4)]], [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearl VI (6)]], and [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearl IX (9)]] with increasing capacity. The Small Sack can hold 2,240 of each item, Medium Sack can hold 6,720 of each item, and Large Sack can hold 20,160 of each item.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Small Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]])|Ender Pearl IV (4) ([[Ender Pearl]])|1x [[Enchanted Leather]] 4x [[Dragon Claw]] 8x [[Crystal Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Crystal Fragment]] x2 ×4 + [[Dragon Claw]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Leather]] → [[Dragon Sack]]|
|Medium Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]])|Ender Pearl VI (6) ([[Ender Pearl]])|1x Small Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]]) 4x [[Summoning Eye]] 16x [[Crystal Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Crystal Fragment]] x4 ×4 + [[Summoning Eye]] ×4 + [[Dragon Sack]] → [[Dragon Sack]]|
|Large Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]])|Ender Pearl IX (9) ([[Ender Pearl]])|1x Medium Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]]) 4x [[Dragon Horn]] 32x [[Crystal Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Crystal Fragment]] x8 ×4 + [[Dragon Horn]] ×4 + [[Dragon Sack]] → [[Dragon Sack]]|
## Usage
The Dragon Sack can store items related to [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]].
|Sack|Capacity|Items|
|-|-|-|
|Small Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]])|2,240|Protector Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]]), Old Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]]), Unstable Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]]), Strong Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]]), Young Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]]), Wise Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]]), Superior Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]]), Holy Dragon Fragment ([[Dragon Fragment]]), [[Mite Gel]], [[Ritual Residue]], [[Crystal Fragment]]|
|Medium Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]])|6,720|(same as above)|
|Large Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]])|20,160|(same as above)|
## Trivia
- When Dragon Sacks were first added, the Small Dragon Sack's crafting recipe had a Small Dragon Sack in the middle instead of [[Enchanted Leather]], which made the item unobtainable. This was fixed shortly after.
## History

|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Medium Dragon Sack.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Large Dragon Sack.|
|Oct 4, 2023|0.19.5|Fixed the textures of Small Dragon Sack and Large Dragon Sack being swapped.|

## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Driftling Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Driftling]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
### Fusion
A Driftling Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||

[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Driftling Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Subterranean Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Subterranean Ruler ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3%–30% more [[Damage]] against [[Subterranean]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Dung
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops
[Dung]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Garden Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 100 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DUNG
Tooltip: Can be spread across a Garden plot with a Sprayonator or used in Pest Traps.; Attracts: Beetle, Fly; UNCOMMON
A Dung is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that can be used with a [[Sprayonator]] to attract [[Pests]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Field Mouse]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv1]|[[Beetle]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Cricket]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Dragonfly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Earthworm]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Firefly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Fly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Locust]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Mite]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Mosquito]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Moth]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Praying Mantis]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Rat]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Slug]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
### Farming
A Dung can also be obtained from the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mooshroom Cow Pet]]'s Bovine Blessing perk, up to a 0.02% (1 in 5000) chance when farming crops.
### Attributes
[[Attributes|Visitor Dung]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants up to 2.5% chance for [[Garden Visitors|Visitors]] to bring Dung, which can be further increased with [[Attributes|Queenly Echo]] attribute.
[[Attributes|Sprayonator Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants 10% chance for [[Pests]] to drop an extra [[Sprayonator|Sprayonator Material]].
## Usage
A Dung can be used with a [[Sprayonator]] to attract [[Beetle|Beetles]] and [[Fly|Flies]] to a sprayed plot.
A Dung can also be used as bait in a [[Pest Trap]], [[Mouse Trap]] or [[Vermin Trap]], allowing them to catch [[Pests]], and increasing the chance to catch a [[Beetle]] or a [[Fly]] by 12x.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Nov 8, 2023|Added Dung.|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Dung as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Nov 14, 2023|0.19.7|Added Dung.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Dung as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Dung can now be obtained from the [[Mooshroom Cow Pet]].|

# Duskbloom
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Duskbloom]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DUSKBLOOM
Tooltip: It turns to neither day nor night, lost somewhere in between.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; UNCOMMON MUTATION
A Duskbloom is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Duskbloom can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 2 [[Moonflower]], 2 [[Shadevine]], 2 [[Sunflower]] and 2 [[Dustgrain]] within 1 block of farmland.
While growing, a Duskbloom needs water and has 8 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it allows for additional items to be found.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Duskblooms also drop 360 (5 stacks plus 40) [[Wheat]], 720 (11 stacks plus 16) [[Moonflower]], 720 [[Sunflower]], and +4,320 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Duskbloom can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 80,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 40 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Duskbloom grants an additional 500 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Duskblooms are used to create a [[Magic Jellybean]], [[Noctilume]], and [[Snoozling]].
### Pet Upgrades
[[Duskbloom]] 4 Duskbloom are needed to upgrade a [[Crow Pet]] from [[Rarity|RARE]] to [[Rarity|EPIC]].
## Gallery
- Duskbloom in the Greenhouse Duskbloom in the [[Greenhouse]]

# Dwarven O's Ore Oats
Aliases: Oats, Ore Oats, Ore oats
Categories: Uncommon items, Items
[Dwarven O's Ore Oats]
Type: Consumable Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: The first 5 times you consume this, gain a permanent +2 [[Ore Fortune]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DWARVEN_OS_ORE_OATS
Tooltip: Consumable; The first 5 times you consume this, gain a permanent +2 Ore Fortune.; Right-click to consume!; UNCOMMON
Dwarven O's Ore Oats are an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Consumables|Consumable]]. It is obtained by completing [[Dwarven Mines|Mining Events]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
Dwarven O's Ore Oats have a chance to drop when participating in [[Dwarven Mines|Mining Events]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Usage
Dwarven O's Ore Oats can be consumed up to 5 times. Each Dwarven O's Ore Oats eaten permanently increases the player's [[Ore Fortune]] by +2 , up to +10 [[Ore Fortune]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Random Message|
|-|-|
|Less than 5 eaten|Mmm, rock and stone!|
|Less than 5 eaten|Mmm, for Karl!|
|Less than 5 eaten|Mmm, they're magically delicious!|
|Less than 5 eaten|Mmm, they're grrreat!|
|Attempting to eat more than 5|Haven't you already had enough?|
|Attempting to eat more than 5|You can't seem to get the packaging off this one...|
|Attempting to eat more than 5|I don't think I should go over my calorie limit for today.|
|Attempting to eat more than 5|I think you should leave some for the rest of the players.|
## Trivia
- The messages for eating Dwarven O's Ore Oats are each references.
  - Both "rock and stone" and "for Karl!" reference Deep Rock Galactic.
  - "They're magically delicious!" is the tagline for Lucky Charms.
  - "They're grrreat!!" is the tagline for Frosted Flakes.

Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Royal reforge when combined with Equipment.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Mining Speed  Mining Fortune; Royal Bonus Earn +15% ᠅ Mithril Powder from Mining Events in the Dwarven Mines.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 14.; UNCOMMON REFORGE STONE
Dwarven Treasure is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Royal]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Equipment]]. It grants [[Mining Speed]] and [[Mining Fortune]].
## Obtaining
### Mining Events
Dwarven Treasure has a 20% (1 in 5) chance to be obtained as a reward from participating in [[Dwarven Mines|Dwarven Events]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Dwarven Treasure can be applied to [[Equipment]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Royal]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Speed]] and [[Mining Fortune]], which scale with the equipment's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XIV (14)]] and can be done with any equipment, regardless of the piece. Equipment that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Royal]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Royal Bonus, which grants +15% ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] from [[Dwarven Mines|Mining Events]] on [[Dwarven Mines]].

- Infiltration ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3–30 [[Farming Fortune]] if your Garden Plot currently has a Pest ([[Pest (Mob Type)]]) in it.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

An Earthworm Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

# Efficient Axe
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Tools, Axes
[Efficient Axe]
Type: Axe ([[Axes]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Drops 5 Planks from chopping Logs.
Source: [[Lumber Merchant]] stock
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Lumber Merchant]]
Buy: 100 [[Coins]]
Sell: 25 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EFFICIENT_AXE
Tooltip: Damage: +25; Drops 5 Planks from chopping Logs.; This item can be reforged!; UNCOMMON AXE
The Efficient Axe is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Axes|Axe]] that will cause 5 planks to drop instead when breaking logs. It can be bought from the [[Lumber Merchant]] for 100 [[Coins]].
## Obtaining
The item is available to be purchased from the [[Lumber Merchant]] located in the [[Village]] for 100 [[Coins]].
### Purchase
|Merchant|Price ([[Coins]]) ×1|
|-|-|
|[[Lumber Merchant]]|100|
## Usage
It can be used to gain more wood planks drop when chopping logs.
- Drops 5 Planks from chopping Logs.
  - This is not affected by [[Foraging Fortune]] or [[Sweep]].
## Notes
- Gained Planks do not count towards player's collection count.
- It's not recommended to use.
- The axe has no effect on [[Fig Log]], [[Mangrove Log]] and [[Helix Log]], and functions like a regular axe instead.

Item ID: EMERALD_RING
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +5; Get +5 coins every minute!; Works while in Accessory Bag!; UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
An Emerald Ring is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] unlocked at [[Emerald|Emerald V (5)]]. Its ability grants the player 5 [[Coins]] every minute while the item is in the player's inventory or [[Accessory Bag]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Emerald V (5) ([[Emerald]])|32x [[Enchanted Emerald]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Emerald]] x4 ×8 → Emerald Ring|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Emerald Artifact]]|None|1x [[Avaricious Chalice]] [[Emerald Ring]] 1x Emerald Ring 36x [[Enchanted Emerald Block]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Emerald Block]] x6 ×6 + Emerald Ring + [[Avaricious Chalice]] → [[Emerald Artifact]]|
## Trivia
- The Emerald Ring produces 7,200 [[Coins]] per day and 2,592,000 [[Coins]] per year.
- The Emerald Ring was used as the icon for the accessories page in the [[Auction House]].
  - Despite this, the Emerald Ring was actually added a year and 5 months after the release of the game and the Auction House.
  - As the texture for the Emerald Ring was changed, the Auction House now uses the texture of the [[Emerald Artifact]], which is the same texture the Emerald Ring used to have.

# Empty Chumcap Bucket
Aliases: Chumcap Bucket, Full Chumcap Bucket

[Empty Chumcap Bucket]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: Empty Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]])
Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EMPTY_CHUMCAP_BUCKET
Tooltip: Place it down next to water.; Sea Creatures slain nearby will drop +1 Chum.; Up to +4 total from buckets!; You gain a good chance to fish up an Agarimoo Sea Creature.; You and other nearby fishers gain +4 Fishing Speed.; Up to +16 from buckets!; ❣ Requires Fishing Skill 15.; UNCOMMON
An Empty Chumcap Bucket is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XV (15)]] to use.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x Empty Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]]) 8x [[Agaricus Chumcap]]|Recipe: [[Agaricus Chumcap]] ×8 + [[Chum Bucket]] → Empty Chumcap Bucket|
## Usage
### Fishing
An Empty Chumcap Bucket can be placed next to [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]]. While any player's Chumcap Bucket is placed, nearby [[Sea Creatures]] killed will drop 1 [[Chum]]. The amount of chum dropped is increased by 1 per nearby Chumcap Bucket, up to 4.
An Empty Chumcap Bucket also gives nearby players +4 [[Fishing Speed]] (up to +16 [[Fishing Speed]]), and gives its owner a chance to catch an [[Agarimoo]].

# Enchanted Blaze Powder
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Craftable items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Blaze Rod Collection, Brewing ingredients, Enchanted

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTED_BLAZE_POWDER
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient Collection Item; UNCOMMON
Enchanted Blaze Powder is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] enchanted item unlocked at [[Blaze Rod|Blaze Rod III (3)]] and is crafted with 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Blaze Rod|Blaze Rods]]. It is used to craft the higher tiers of the [[Blaze Minion]] and [[Enchanted Blaze Rod|Enchanted Blaze Rods]]. It can also be used to craft the [[Fire Talisman]], the [[Blaze Belt]] and to upgrade the [[Phoenix Pet]] at [[Kat]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Blaze Rod III (3) ([[Blaze Rod]])|160x [[Blaze Rod]]|Recipe: [[Blaze Rod]] x32 ×7 → Enchanted Blaze Powder|
### [[Inferno Demonlord]]
|Tier|Amount|Rarity|
|-|-|-|
|Tier I|[[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] 1 Enchanted Blaze Powder|21.26%|
|Tier II|[[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] 2–3 Enchanted Blaze Powder|13.171%|
|Tier III|[[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] 4–8 Enchanted Blaze Powder|12.5581%|
|Tier IV|[[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] 16–40 Enchanted Blaze Powder|11.7269%|
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv508]|[[Ashfang]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv508]|[[Ashfang]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
### Lava Fishing
Fishing in any lava has a base 0.122% chance of awarding [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] Enchanted Blaze Powder. This chance can be increased to 0.5122% with [[Fishing|Fishing L (50)]] and [[Luck of the Sea|Luck of the Sea VI]].

#### [[Strength Potion]] (III)
Recipe: Enchanted Blaze Powder → [[Strength Potion]], [[Strength Potion]], [[Strength Potion]]
## Trivia
- Despite the name, [[Blaze Powder]] is actually not used to make Enchanted Blaze Powder.

# Enchanted Carrot on a Stick
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Craftable items, Carrot Collection, Enchanted
[Enchanted Carrot on a Stick]
Type: Enchanted item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Carrot V (5) ([[Carrot]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 10,240 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 10,240x (160 stacks (10,240 items total)) [[Carrot]] 3x [[Stick]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTED_CARROT_STICK
Tooltip: Increases the speed of the creature you are riding when holding.; UNCOMMON
An Enchanted Carrot on a Stick is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item. It is unlocked at [[Carrot|Carrot V (5)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Carrot V (5) ([[Carrot]])|3x [[Stick]] 64x [[Enchanted Carrot]]|Recipe: [[Stick]] ×3 + [[Enchanted Carrot]] x32 ×2 → Enchanted Carrot on a Stick|
## Usage
While held, an Enchanted Carrot on a Stick increases the speed of mounted creatures that the player is riding. However, due to both the [[Horse Pet]] and [[Pig Pet]] no longer being mounts, it is practically useless.
## Trivia
- As of 0.24.5, there are only two rideable mount [[Pets]] in the game: the [[Armadillo Pet]] and the [[Rock Pet]]. However, they are not affected by an Enchanted Carrot on a Stick.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Enchanted Carrot on a Stick.|

Enchanted Charcoal is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Minion Fuel]] unlocked at [[Coal|Coal V (5)]]. It increases the speed of [[Minions]] by 20% for 36 hours.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Coal V (5) ([[Coal]])|32 Log ([[Wood (disambiguation)]]) (Vanilla) 128 (2 stacks) [[Coal]]|Recipe: [[Coal]] x32 ×6 + [[Oak Log]] x32/[[Birch Log]] x32/[[Spruce Log]] x32/[[Dark Oak Log]] x32/[[Acacia Log]] x32/[[Jungle Log]] x32 → Enchanted Charcoal|
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv144]|[[Wither Skeleton]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
## Usage
### Minion Fuel
Enchanted Charcoal can be used as a fuel source for [[Minions]]. It increases the efficiency by 20% for 36 hours. Enchanted Charcoal can be placed in all [[Minions]].
## Tips
- Since it only requires 2 stacks of coal to make, and it is not consumed very quickly, this is a very good option for early-mid game fuel and boosting minion production.

The Enchanted Dandelion is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item obtained from crafting 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Flowers|Dandelions]]. They are not part of any [[Collections|Collection]], and have no requirement to unlock the recipe. Their only uses are to upgrade the [[Flower Minion]] and [[Pablo]]'s quest.

### Crafting

|-|-|-|-|
|Flower Minion V ([[Flower Minion]])|None|1x Flower Minion IV ([[Flower Minion]]) [[Enchanted Dandelion]] 8x Enchanted Dandelion|Recipe: Enchanted Dandelion ×8 + [[Flower Minion]] → [[Flower Minion]]|
|Flower Minion VI ([[Flower Minion]])|None|1x Flower Minion V ([[Flower Minion]]) [[Enchanted Dandelion]] 24x Enchanted Dandelion|Recipe: Enchanted Dandelion x3 ×8 + [[Flower Minion]] → [[Flower Minion]]|
|Flower Minion VII ([[Flower Minion]])|None|1x Flower Minion VI ([[Flower Minion]]) [[Enchanted Dandelion]] 64x Enchanted Dandelion|Recipe: Enchanted Dandelion x8 ×8 + [[Flower Minion]] → [[Flower Minion]]|
|Flower Minion VIII ([[Flower Minion]])|None|1x Flower Minion VII ([[Flower Minion]]) [[Enchanted Dandelion]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) Enchanted Dandelion|Recipe: Enchanted Dandelion x16 ×8 + [[Flower Minion]] → [[Flower Minion]]|
|Flower Minion IX ([[Flower Minion]])|None|1x Flower Minion VIII ([[Flower Minion]]) [[Enchanted Dandelion]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) Enchanted Dandelion|Recipe: Enchanted Dandelion x32 ×8 + [[Flower Minion]] → [[Flower Minion]]|
|Flower Minion X ([[Flower Minion]])|None|1x Flower Minion IX ([[Flower Minion]]) [[Enchanted Dandelion]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Enchanted Dandelion|Recipe: Enchanted Dandelion x64 ×8 + [[Flower Minion]] → [[Flower Minion]]|
### Machine Fuel
The Enchanted Dandelion can be also used as a [[Machine Fuel]] in [[Drills]], adding 360 Fuel.
## Trivia
- Before the addition of Enchanted Dandelion to the [[Bazaar]], it would take 250 days to upgrade the [[Flower Minion]] from Tier V to Tier X if the player purchased the maximum of 640 (10 stacks) Dandelions every day from the [[Builder]], although this could be sped up by buying 6,400 (100 stacks) Dandelion per day while [[Diaz]] is mayor. The only other ways to obtain them is from farming them from the front of the [[Flower House]], using [[Bone Meal]], or trading with other players.

## Obtaining
### Crafting

|-|-|-|

A [[Campfire Badge|Campfire Scion Badge]] can be upgraded to a [[Campfire Badge|Campfire God Badge]] by giving [[Ryan]] [[Enchanted Jungle Log]] 1,500 (23 stacks plus 28) Enchanted Jungle Logs upon completing [[Trials of Fire|Trials of Fire XXI (21)]].
